- **triage-orchestrator**: Autonomous Jira issue triage - bulk categorization, label/component/priority suggestions, dedupe detection, linking issues to PRs and Confluence docs, batch transitions, and structured epic/sub-task creation. Triggers: 'triage incoming bugs', 'categorize unprocessed issues', 'find duplicates in this project', 'create epic with sub-tasks', 'batch-assign these issues'. ([Acendas/atlassian-suite](https://github.com/Acendas/atlassian-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-orchestrator.md))
- **triage-orchestrator**: Outer-loop triage orchestrator. Consolidates the discovery engine (discover-work.sh / loop-triage, T-017), the Operating-Loop telemetry roll-up (rollup-runs.sh, T-020), and cross-run failure clusters (cluster-failures.sh, T-044) into ONE de-duplicated, BUDGET-capped triage proposal file for a human to act on. Propose-only and manual-trigger — never edits the board. Use at the start of a cycle to decide what to pick up next. ([RipsawJP/shell-team](https://github.com/RipsawJP/shell-team/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-orchestrator.md))
- **triage-orchestrator**: 設定した条件に合う Coverity の警告を、複数のエージェントで並列に処置し、同じ原因の CID を統合して、すべての結果を別のエージェントに検証させる。`copilot --agent` で起動したメインの会話として使う。 ([xclang-brain/plugin-market-place](https://github.com/xclang-brain/plugin-market-place/blob/HEAD/plugins/coverity-triage-kit/com.github.copilot/agents/triage-orchestrator.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **triage-product**: Read-only triage perspective for incoming tasks. Challenges the user intent — who actually uses this, what problem it solves, what's in MVP, what edge cases break it, what success looks like. Invoked by /triage in parallel with triage-architect and triage-engineer. Returns a structured brief — never modifies files. ([natanrotta/claude-setup](https://github.com/natanrotta/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-product.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **triage-project**: The `[Triage] Project sync` workflow mirrors [triage state labels](triage-labels.md) into an organization Project's `Status` field. Labels are authoritative; dragging a card does not change them. ([homarr-labs/homarr](https://github.com/homarr-labs/homarr/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-project.md) · ★5.4k)
- **triage-proposer**: Reads ONE sanitised triage artifact and returns a single JSON proposal: labels from closed lists, a two-sentence summary, the quoted error and a section hint. Holds no tools but Read. Spawned by /triage, once per issue — never invoked directly for feature work. ([Zalaras/muster](https://github.com/Zalaras/muster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-proposer.md) · ★3)
- **triage-protocol**: The judgment layer between "an issue exists" and "the human approves it". Triage reads every untriaged open issue (no `fleet:` / `human:` label), judges it against the target repo's **objectives-path** and the filing rules, and produces a verdict the human acts on in one read. Two modes share the same inputs and judgment: ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-protocol.md) · ★10)
- **triage-qa**: Cross-boundary QA for alert-triage-agent — compares contracts across module seams (journal events vs eval assertions vs Pydantic models vs prompts), runs make check and make eval, executes the agent end-to-end. Use after each implemented module (incremental QA), not once at the end. ([baodq97/alert-triage-agent](https://github.com/baodq97/alert-triage-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-qa.md))
- **triage-quality**: You are a fast triage reviewer. Scan PR #&lt;PR_NUMBER&gt; in &lt;REPO_SLUG&gt; for Critical and Important issues only. Skip observations, style, and conventions. ([oleg-koval/agent-skills](https://github.com/oleg-koval/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/olko-github-pr/skills/lekker-review/references/agents/triage-quality.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **triage-rank**: Orders candidate groups by a fixed key built from evidence. Shows the key values beside each rank. Produces no numeric score. ([portarj482-lang/Cantina](https://github.com/portarj482-lang/Cantina/blob/HEAD/global/agents/triage-rank.md))
- **triage-ranker**: Severity ranker for one confirmed triage finding. Spawn with a repo path, deployment context, and finding fields — not for general delegation. ([air-gapped/skills](https://github.com/air-gapped/skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-ranker.md) · ★6)
- **triage-reader**: Reads untrusted text (an email, a web page, an issue, a ticket, a chat message, a vendor reply) and returns a small JSON summary of what it says. Has no secrets, no network and no write access; only Read. Use whenever outside text must be understood before a privileged step, so that instructions hidden in that text cannot steer the engineer. ([jcallahanp/Autonomy-Research](https://github.com/jcallahanp/Autonomy-Research/blob/HEAD/engineer/plugin/agents/triage-reader.md))

- **tribridge-delegate**: Hands one well-specified, bulky unit of work to another AI agent (Claude Code, Codex or Antigravity/Gemini — never the one running it) through tribridge and returns the other agent's digest. Use it proactively for above-break-even work — scaffolding, exhaustive test generation, mechanical migrations, long-context reading distilled to a digest, web research via Gemini — so the bulk is not generated by the calling model. It does not verify and never claims success: the caller verifies. Do NOT use it for small, quick or judgement-heavy tasks; do those directly. ([JoakoVRD-Designer/tribridge](https://github.com/JoakoVRD-Designer/tribridge/blob/HEAD/agents/tribridge-delegate.md))
- **tribunal**: Six-persona quality review of tone-extraction outputs and voice-spec synthesis. Use to stress-test fingerprint hypotheses, move definitions, and synthesized voice specs for definitional clarity, falsifiability, field-vs-personal separation, operationalizability, hidden-judgment detection, and supervisor-relationship fit. ([ozrwayne/phd-tone-system](https://github.com/ozrwayne/phd-tone-system/blob/HEAD/agents/tribunal.md) · ★1)
- **tribunal-actor**: Performs a task end-to-end under Tribunal supervision. Receives the task description and, on retry iterations, the prior iteration's jury verdicts and Meta-Judge feedback. Output is the work itself (code changes, an analysis, a refactor plan) rendered as the final assistant message — no JSON wrapping, no scoring; the Judges do that next. ([onlooker-community/ecosystem](https://github.com/onlooker-community/ecosystem/blob/HEAD/plugins/tribunal/agents/tribunal-actor.md))
- **tribunal-judge-adversarial**: Devil's-advocate Tribunal judge. Actively tries to break the Actor's work — edge cases, empty inputs, concurrent callers, partial failures, version drift, assumptions that are not stated. Pairs well with tribunal-judge-standard to balance optimistic and pessimistic scoring. Emits TribunalVerdictPayload as the final message. Read-only (Bash allowed only to run existing test suites — do not modify code). ([onlooker-community/ecosystem](https://github.com/onlooker-community/ecosystem/blob/HEAD/plugins/tribunal/agents/tribunal-judge-adversarial.md))
- **tribunal-judge-security**: Security-focused Tribunal judge. Scores Actor output through a vulnerability lens — injection, auth, secrets, unsafe shell, path traversal, deserialization, SSRF, race conditions on shared resources. Off by default; opt in by adding "security" to judge_types for security-sensitive code. Emits TribunalVerdictPayload as the final message. Read-only. ([onlooker-community/ecosystem](https://github.com/onlooker-community/ecosystem/blob/HEAD/plugins/tribunal/agents/tribunal-judge-security.md))
- **tribunal-judge-standard**: Scores Actor output against the active rubric on correctness, completeness, and clarity. Emits a single TribunalVerdictPayload JSON object as the final message — no prose around it. Read-only: you evaluate, you do not edit. Designed for the general case (refactors, docs, analysis, most code changes). Use the security or adversarial judge for those specific lenses. ([onlooker-community/ecosystem](https://github.com/onlooker-community/ecosystem/blob/HEAD/plugins/tribunal/agents/tribunal-judge-standard.md))
